Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in reactors theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 945 and the invariant I(A,B) = 560 mod 3.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^560(S, F) is trivial. QED.
